  Plan: |-
    1. Run all source-tree, provenance, inventory, type, lint, unit, browser, ODT fixture and static build gates on the implemented slice.
    2. Enumerate each currently reachable module operation and residual unverified/divergent record; compare contract, defaults, behavior and source responsibility against pinned LibreOffice source or an explicit browser exception. Record atomic evidence without promoting whole modules from narrow tests.
    3. Repair every demonstrated browser-relevant mismatch in the matching upstream-shaped owner, add differential/source-backed assertions, and update only inventory/provenance data.
    4. Re-run the full gates and review every remaining record by hand. Close only if all implemented browser-relevant operations have defensible evidence and exceptions; otherwise preserve truthful findings and continue the audit.

    User verification instruction2026-10-04 is persistent:run tests once with vendor/libreoffice-reference unavailable;do not run source-present suites and then repeat them absent. Split existing npm verify gates without weakening acceptance:non-test format/lint/type/dependency/local static/JSDoc/file-size checks;one vendor-absent npm run test plus owned script/resource tests and npm run test:e2e,with restore in finally;source-dependent resource generation --check/source-tree/provenance/invariants/parity CLI audits separately after restoration. No helper scripts/Python/source/native copies in Agentplane;results/hashes/conclusions only. This changes orchestration only,not goal scope or semantic criteria.

    1. All existing verify gates pass on final code with split orchestration:format/lint/type/dependency/local static build/JSDoc/file-size checks;one test/coverage/resource/ODT/browser pass while vendor/libreoffice-reference is unavailable and restored in finally;then separate source-dependent resource generation --check/source-tree/provenance/inventory/invariant/parity CLI audits after restoration. Never run tests with upstream present and repeat them absent. Preserve100%app/inventory coverage and semantic criteria.
    2. Every implemented browser-relevant operation has a pinned source file/symbol or explicit browser exception, compatible type/default/ownership and assertion-level or differential behavior evidence; no unsupported native operation is silently treated as implemented.
    3. All residual unverified/divergent runtime and command records are reviewed individually and either resolved with evidence, repaired, or explicitly scoped as browser exception; no blanket module promotion.
    4. Source paths and inventory data match actual files and exports; task-scoped diff and tracked checkout are clean.

  Findings: |-
    Scoped parity fixes completed on 2026-09-24: SwFlowFrame paragraph spacing now retains both margins for mixed contextual-spacing flags when PARA_SPACE_MAX is enabled; SwTransferable now copies and cuts selections consisting only of paragraph boundaries while still rejecting coincident endpoints. Command: npm run verify. Result: pass. Evidence: 488/488 application unit tests, 98/98 inventory tests, 14/14 browser tests, 100% required coverage, static build and source checks passed. Scope: these two Writer behaviors and their regression tests. The broader operation-level Stage 8 audit remains in progress and is not declared complete.

    Progress on 2026-09-30: sixteen bounded corrections were completed in separate tasks, without closing the overall audit: 202609301433-Z6WPSC (d17c8bd, signed paragraph side margins and unsnapped decrease); 202609301437-ET663N (7ea0a3f, Put out-of-range filtering); 202609301452-WQ0C2J (ba5d182, clone parent/state and cross-pool contracts); 202609301502-ZN5HAJ (9935632, PutSet invalid-as-default and disabled filtering); 202609301514-CTCC8R (a27de86, explicit state setter range filtering); 202609301526-CHAQ5Y (366dc44, frame margin/spacing classes moved unchanged to the corresponding frmitems owner, twenty direct consumers and existing tests relocated); 202609301539-JQNMYH (693a211, SwAttrSet polymorphic Clone preserves same-pool Writer type and pinned generic/Writer destination branches); 202609301551-S2X2YK (380c4b5, source-style parent reference assignment and inherited pool-default delegation); 202609301603-AEE039 (1055b57, poolitem-owned disabled singleton, null Clone contract and disabled Get identity); 202609301617-7M8MJP (1b562ee, native single-map SfxItemSet value/state storage and distinct poolitem-owned INVALID singleton); 202609301639-P4J3MC (9fab51e, signed sal_Int32 tab positions/direct default distances and negative ODF tab import); 202609301651-QBNFD9 (982cc88, source-order Default tab sequence selection before canonical sorting); 202609301706-8FPY82 (cfdbf40, native XML dispatch/tab/unit source ownership with unchanged body comparison and direct consumer imports). 202609301724-XZQVDC (4d87aab, native absent-position zero and recognized-only alignment override defaults) is also closed. 202609301734-F5X9J6 (b461207, source-owned native tab measure pipeline) is also closed. 202609301804-02N2JC (c2a47b2, native per-tab leaf value and context-reference ownership) is also closed. Each task records source-backed assertions and full verification. Latest npm run verify: pass, 584 app tests, 109 inventory tests, 19 browser tests, 100% required coverage and all other gates. A transient TXT-save timing failure in ET663N passed isolated and complete retries without changing save behavior; its diagnostics are retained in that task. Deliberate save/open/recovery decisions remain intact. The two previously recorded explicit-state and source-owner follow-ups are now resolved. The SwAttrSet Clone follow-up is now resolved with direct assertions for all destination branches, including the unusual pinned empty foreign Writer-pool result. Parent assignment/default ownership is now resolved with distinct child/parent/grandparent pool assertions. The disabled Get follow-up is resolved, including WhichId zero, null Clone, pointer identity, clone-state preservation and non-persistence assertions. The temporary dual-map item-set representation is now resolved: one WhichId-to-item PoolItemMap stores ordinary values and distinct INVALID/DISABLED singleton pointers, with source-style transitions and preserved clone, PutSet and sorted SET-only browser projection contracts. The first full run identified two existing ODT corruption tests depending on the removed private items field; they were adapted with unchanged rejection assertions, and the complete rerun passed. Task-local diagnostics are retained. The signed-tab follow-up is now resolved with endpoint/ordering/replacement/clone/real Writer-pool codec assertions and real negative direct/inherited-style ODT input/export/reimport evidence. The direct setter remains distinct from native UNO PutValue negative-distance validation. Wider XML behavior/contracts are now honestly marked unverified rather than treating bounded sequence tests as whole-module parity. The Default-tab selection follow-up is now resolved. Six literal sequence fixtures verify original source order, first Default exclusivity, omission of later Defaults, empty/signed sequences and retained fields across direct/inherited-style import and canonical export/reimport. The contradictory previous middle-Default assertion now follows pinned behavior. The XML responsibility split is now resolved: the text-property dispatcher creates SvxXMLTabStopImportContext from style/xmltabi.ts, and shared conversion lives in core/xmluconv.ts with every direct consumer updated and no compatibility re-export. AST comparison proves four context member implementations and the complete converter unchanged; 27 existing focused tests and the complete verification passed unchanged. Provenance now records 194 runtime modules (118 mapped), with wider contracts/defaults honestly unverified. The tab initialization/override follow-up is resolved in XZQVDC: 12 literal cases verify bare/absent position defaults, absent/empty/unknown/case/whitespace types retaining LEFT, recognized types, signed positions and later-Default omission across direct/inherited ODT import/export/reimport. Existing contradictory rejection assertion now expects native LEFT while preserving position and leader fields. Full verification passed at 578/109/19 and 100% coverage; registered save/open/recovery decisions are intact. The native tab measure follow-up is resolved in F5X9J6: SAX Converter owns parsing for existing Twip/MM100 targets, SvXMLUnitConverter delegates configured conversion with nullable failure and default/caller bounds, and tab imports retain native MM100 until signed integer conversion at Writer item application. Import-only XMLTabStop/XMLParagraphImportProperties contracts distinguish these values from twip export data. Twenty literal direct/inherited ODT cases verify native grammar/units, positive/negative two-stage quantization, signed rounding, saturation, failed-position zero fallback and replacement of colliding converted positions through export/reimport. Ninety-five comparisons against compiled unmodified pinned C++ parsing/measure functions pass; the harness supplies platform aliases and native o3tl ratios for these two targets. The first comparison exposed signed UTF-8 char whitespace behavior in the actual fast-attribute std::string_view, now reproduced and documented. Static-only class lint and missing test callback JSDoc were corrected without validator/config changes; complete final verification passes at 583/109/19 and 100% coverage. Provenance maps 195 runtime modules (119 mapped), with broader semantic statuses unverified. Strict legacy helper callers retain their unaudited syntax/error policy but delegate numeric conversion to SAX; they still need individual source review. Other native converter APIs/targets and UTF-16 overload remain unimplemented/unverified. The per-tab ownership follow-up is resolved in 02N2JC: internal SvxXMLTabStopContext_Impl owns constructor parsing and the readonly MM100 property; the parent stores/returns leaf context references and selects values through getTabStop. Pinned-baseline AST comparison proves attribute parsing, value initializer and Default-selection predicates unchanged. A new real direct/inherited ODT cycle verifies that known and unknown nested leaf subtrees cannot alter tab fields, add stops, change sibling selection or append document text. Focused 11 tests and complete final verification pass at 584/109/19 and 100% coverage. Exact native base-class/global dispatcher contracts remain open: pinned xmlictxt.cxx returns null for child handlers, and fastparser.cxx callStartElement skips null-context subtrees including descendants. Local core/xmlimp.ts still rejects known null child handlers. The existing ignore-context base preserves tab-leaf subtree outcomes while this global policy is audited; it is not declared a registered intentional deviation. Restore native null-handler dispatch only with separate source review of explicit unimplemented-feature guards and diagnostic/root contracts; no broad unsupported operation may be silently represented as implemented. Next bounded functional correction, now directly source-backed: XMLImpSpanContext_Impl constructor in pinned xmloff/source/text/txtparai.cxx lines 1263-1303 always constructs the span but creates a style hint only when aStyleName is nonempty; its characters method inserts text even without a hint, and its child factory delegates normal inline handling. Local createInlineContext in xmloff/source/text/txtparai.ts maps omitted TEXT_STYLE_NAME to an empty name then unconditionally resolveTextStyle, throwing Unsupported ODF text style for absent/empty style. Existing unsupported-structure fixtures incorrectly expect bare and nested unstyled spans to fail. Restore absent/empty span style as no character-style delta, retaining effective inherited formatting and active hyperlink, with plain/nested/styled-parent/control-character/hyperlink and real ODT cycle assertions. Unknown named-style fallback needs separate source review rather than conflating it with absent style. No registered intentional deviation was found for these contracts. Global null-context handling, legacy measure-helper caller syntax/failure policies and generated-stop constructor uniqueness/domain contracts remain open for individual review. Registered save/open/recovery decisions are untouched. Other unverified operations and UI remain open; whole-module or overall parity is not claimed.

Keep cyclic guard, paragraph/list policies, family table/display-name identity, generic null handling and broader hint architecture separate until individually reviewed. Registered save/open/recovery decisions remain intact; the entire runtime/UI audit is incomplete.

    Progress: eighteenth bounded correction 202609301846-ZBRNAK (32787845518c) is DONE. Eleven new context cases and eleven literal real ODT package cycles verify exact unknown/case/whitespace names, paragraph/character inheritance, optional properties, explicit false overrides, nested scope restoration, controls and hyperlinks. Valid named parent styles are in styles.xml and automatic children in content.xml, matching native SetStyleAndAttrs ownership. The unknown/property-less character-style follow-up is resolved within this bounded contract; the cycle guard remains unaudited. Focused 18 tests and complete final npm run verify pass: 588 app, 109 inventory, 19 browser, 100% required coverage, all static/source gates and semanticViolationCount 0. No config/schema/generator/validator weakening. Wider table identity, display names, hint ownership and all other unverified runtime/UI operations remain open. Next separate correction is directly source-backed: StyleIndexCompareByName and SvXMLStylesContext_Impl::FindStyleChildContext in pinned xmloff/source/style/xmlstyle.cxx lines 157-165 and 246-280 index and compare family plus name; identical paragraph and character names occupy distinct style identities. XMLTextImportHelper::SetStyleAndAttrs likewise requests a family-specific lookup. Local SwXMLImport.styles in sw/source/filter/xml/xmlimp.ts is Map<string,OdfStyleDefinition>, getStyle(name) has no family argument, and registerStyle rejects any already-used name. Local resolveParagraphStyle/getStyle and resolveTextStyle/getStyle therefore cannot support same-name paragraph/text definitions; the built-in paragraph path can even read a text definition. Restore family-scoped storage, registration and every style resolution call plus named paragraph style application without aliases or fallback to another family. Verify same-name direct/parent and built-in Standard/Heading cases through source-derived contexts and literal real ODT import/export/reimport; preserve separate same-family duplicate handling until native duplicate selection is audited. Common/automatic container identity and display-name mapping remain distinct follow-ups, not registered intentional deviations. Registered save/open/recovery decisions stay intact. Overall audit remains incomplete.

Local SwXMLDocContext still constructs the same XMLStylesContext(target) for both containers; SwXMLImport registration merges every definition into one table per family, so legal common/automatic name collisions are rejected and parent lookup can incorrectly consume an automatic definition. Separate per-container source ownership/active references and native automatic-first/direct versus named-parent lookup; account for styles.xml and content.xml automatic context replacement rather than merely introducing one merged automatic table. Verify equal named/automatic identities, native parent scope, automatic override precedence and stream-specific references through real packages. Display-name mapping, same-family duplicate selection, cycles/defaults, legacy measure callers, null-context handling, tab constructor/domain contracts and other unverified runtime/UI remain separate audits. ## Summary

Close implemented runtime parity audit

Stage 8: run full checks and operation-level review of implemented browser-relevant runtime; record evidence and remaining explicit exceptions

## Scope

- In scope: Stage 8: run full checks and operation-level review of implemented browser-relevant runtime; record evidence and remaining explicit exceptions.
- Out of scope: unrelated refactors not required for "Close implemented runtime parity audit".

## Plan

1. Run all source-tree, provenance, inventory, type, lint, unit, browser, ODT fixture and static build gates on the implemented slice.
2. Enumerate each currently reachable module operation and residual unverified/divergent record; compare contract, defaults, behavior and source responsibility against pinned LibreOffice source or an explicit browser exception. Record atomic evidence without promoting whole modules from narrow tests.
3. Repair every demonstrated browser-relevant mismatch in the matching upstream-shaped owner, add differential/source-backed assertions, and update only inventory/provenance data.
4. Re-run the full gates and review every remaining record by hand. Close only if all implemented browser-relevant operations have defensible evidence and exceptions; otherwise preserve truthful findings and continue the audit.

User verification instruction2026-10-04 is persistent:run tests once with vendor/libreoffice-reference unavailable;do not run source-present suites and then repeat them absent. Split existing npm verify gates without weakening acceptance:non-test format/lint/type/dependency/local static/JSDoc/file-size checks;one vendor-absent npm run test plus owned script/resource tests and npm run test:e2e,with restore in finally;source-dependent resource generation --check/source-tree/provenance/invariants/parity CLI audits separately after restoration. No helper scripts/Python/source/native copies in Agentplane;results/hashes/conclusions only. This changes orchestration only,not goal scope or semantic criteria.

## Verify Steps

1. All existing verify gates pass on final code with split orchestration:format/lint/type/dependency/local static build/JSDoc/file-size checks;one test/coverage/resource/ODT/browser pass while vendor/libreoffice-reference is unavailable and restored in finally;then separate source-dependent resource generation --check/source-tree/provenance/inventory/invariant/parity CLI audits after restoration. Never run tests with upstream present and repeat them absent. Preserve100%app/inventory coverage and semantic criteria.
2. Every implemented browser-relevant operation has a pinned source file/symbol or explicit browser exception, compatible type/default/ownership and assertion-level or differential behavior evidence; no unsupported native operation is silently treated as implemented.
3. All residual unverified/divergent runtime and command records are reviewed individually and either resolved with evidence, repaired, or explicitly scoped as browser exception; no blanket module promotion.
4. Source paths and inventory data match actual files and exports; task-scoped diff and tracked checkout are clean.

## Verification

<!-- BEGIN VERIFICATION RESULTS -->
<!-- END VERIFICATION RESULTS -->

## Rollback Plan

- Revert task-related commit(s).
- Re-run required checks to confirm rollback safety.

## Findings

Scoped parity fixes completed on 2026-09-24: SwFlowFrame paragraph spacing now retains both margins for mixed contextual-spacing flags when PARA_SPACE_MAX is enabled; SwTransferable now copies and cuts selections consisting only of paragraph boundaries while still rejecting coincident endpoints. Command: npm run verify. Result: pass. Evidence: 488/488 application unit tests, 98/98 inventory tests, 14/14 browser tests, 100% required coverage, static build and source checks passed. Scope: these two Writer behaviors and their regression tests. The broader operation-level Stage 8 audit remains in progress and is not declared complete.

Progress on 2026-09-30: sixteen bounded corrections were completed in separate tasks, without closing the overall audit: 202609301433-Z6WPSC (d17c8bd, signed paragraph side margins and unsnapped decrease); 202609301437-ET663N (7ea0a3f, Put out-of-range filtering); 202609301452-WQ0C2J (ba5d182, clone parent/state and cross-pool contracts); 202609301502-ZN5HAJ (9935632, PutSet invalid-as-default and disabled filtering); 202609301514-CTCC8R (a27de86, explicit state setter range filtering); 202609301526-CHAQ5Y (366dc44, frame margin/spacing classes moved unchanged to the corresponding frmitems owner, twenty direct consumers and existing tests relocated); 202609301539-JQNMYH (693a211, SwAttrSet polymorphic Clone preserves same-pool Writer type and pinned generic/Writer destination branches); 202609301551-S2X2YK (380c4b5, source-style parent reference assignment and inherited pool-default delegation); 202609301603-AEE039 (1055b57, poolitem-owned disabled singleton, null Clone contract and disabled Get identity); 202609301617-7M8MJP (1b562ee, native single-map SfxItemSet value/state storage and distinct poolitem-owned INVALID singleton); 202609301639-P4J3MC (9fab51e, signed sal_Int32 tab positions/direct default distances and negative ODF tab import); 202609301651-QBNFD9 (982cc88, source-order Default tab sequence selection before canonical sorting); 202609301706-8FPY82 (cfdbf40, native XML dispatch/tab/unit source ownership with unchanged body comparison and direct consumer imports). 202609301724-XZQVDC (4d87aab, native absent-position zero and recognized-only alignment override defaults) is also closed. 202609301734-F5X9J6 (b461207, source-owned native tab measure pipeline) is also closed. 202609301804-02N2JC (c2a47b2, native per-tab leaf value and context-reference ownership) is also closed. Each task records source-backed assertions and full verification. Latest npm run verify: pass, 584 app tests, 109 inventory tests, 19 browser tests, 100% required coverage and all other gates. A transient TXT-save timing failure in ET663N passed isolated and complete retries without changing save behavior; its diagnostics are retained in that task. Deliberate save/open/recovery decisions remain intact. The two previously recorded explicit-state and source-owner follow-ups are now resolved. The SwAttrSet Clone follow-up is now resolved with direct assertions for all destination branches, including the unusual pinned empty foreign Writer-pool result. Parent assignment/default ownership is now resolved with distinct child/parent/grandparent pool assertions. The disabled Get follow-up is resolved, including WhichId zero, null Clone, pointer identity, clone-state preservation and non-persistence assertions. The temporary dual-map item-set representation is now resolved: one WhichId-to-item PoolItemMap stores ordinary values and distinct INVALID/DISABLED singleton pointers, with source-style transitions and preserved clone, PutSet and sorted SET-only browser projection contracts. The first full run identified two existing ODT corruption tests depending on the removed private items field; they were adapted with unchanged rejection assertions, and the complete rerun passed. Task-local diagnostics are retained. The signed-tab follow-up is now resolved with endpoint/ordering/replacement/clone/real Writer-pool codec assertions and real negative direct/inherited-style ODT input/export/reimport evidence. The direct setter remains distinct from native UNO PutValue negative-distance validation. Wider XML behavior/contracts are now honestly marked unverified rather than treating bounded sequence tests as whole-module parity. The Default-tab selection follow-up is now resolved. Six literal sequence fixtures verify original source order, first Default exclusivity, omission of later Defaults, empty/signed sequences and retained fields across direct/inherited-style import and canonical export/reimport. The contradictory previous middle-Default assertion now follows pinned behavior.
